```
class Solution:
    def flipgame(self, fronts: List[int], backs: List[int]) -> int:
        same = {fronts[i] for i in range(len(fronts)) if fronts[i] == backs[i]}
        ans = 10000
        for x in itertools.chain(fronts, backs):
            if x not in same:
                ans = min(ans, x)
        return ans if ans != 10000 else 0
```
Yes. Because any number that appears on both sides of same card cannot be good? Wait if number x appears on same card both sides, it's on front and back of that card. If choose that card, x is on front and back. If choose another card? Need x on front/back of some card? Let's understand.

```
class Solution:
    def flipgame(self, fronts: List[int], backs: List[int]) -> int:
        same = set()
        for i in range(len(fronts)):
            if fronts[i] == backs[i]:
                same.add(fronts[i])
        ans = float('inf')
        for num in fronts + backs:
            if num not in same:
                ans = min(ans, num)
        return ans if ans != float('inf') else 0
```
Yes.

from typing import List

class Solution:
    def flipgame(self, fronts: List[int], backs: List[int]) -> int:
        bad = {i for i, j in zip(fronts, backs) if i == j}
        for i in sorted(set(fronts + backs)):
            if i in bad:
                continue
            return i
        return 0
```

from typing import List

class Solution:
    def flipgame(self, fronts: List[int], backs: List[int]) -> int:
        bad = {i for i, j in zip(fronts, backs) if i == j}
        for i in sorted(set(fronts + backs)):
            if i in bad:
                continue
            return i
        return 0
```

If condition is "front not on back of any card", then choose card with front 1 no flip? But front 1 is not on back of any card (backs [2,3]), so 1 good. So known solution matches "front not on back of any card", not "back not on front of any card". Let's verify with example fronts=[1,2,4,4,7], backs=[1,3,4,1,3], answer 2. If condition "front not on back of any card": To make front=2, choose card2 no flip? Card2 front=2, back=3. Is 2 on back of any card? Backs [1,3,4,1,3], no 2. So 2 good. If condition "back not on front of any card": To make back=2, flip card2, front=3, back=2. Is 2 on front of any card? Fronts after flipping [1,3,4,4,7], no 2. So 2 good too. Both conditions yield 2 in example.

class Solution:
    def flipgame(self, fronts: List[int], backs: List[int]) -> int:
        same = {fronts[i] for i in range(len(fronts)) if fronts[i] == backs[i]}
        ans = 0
        for n in sorted(set(fronts + backs)):
            if n not in same:
                ans = n
                break
        return ans
```

```
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def flipgame(self, fronts: List[int], backs: List[int]) -> int:
        bad = set()
        for f, b in zip(fronts, backs):
            if f == b:
                bad.add(f)
        for x in sorted(set(fronts + backs)):
            if x not in bad:
                return x
        return 0
```

from typing import List

class Solution:
    def flipgame(self, fronts: List[int], backs: List[int]) -> int:
        bad = {f for f, b in zip(fronts, backs) if f == b}
        for x in sorted(set(fronts + backs)):
            if x not in bad:
                return x
        return 0
```

        for x in sorted(set(fronts + backs)):
            if x not in bad:

        return 0

```
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def flipgame(self, fronts: List[int], backs: List[int]) -> int:
        bad = {f for f, b in zip(fronts, backs) if f == b}
        for x in sorted(set(fronts + backs)):
            if x not in bad:
                return x
        return 0
```

=== FIXED CODE ===
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def flipgame(self, fronts: List[int], backs: List[int]) -> int:
        bad = {f for f, b in zip(fronts, backs) if f == b}
        for x in sorted(set(fronts + backs)):
            if x not in bad:
                return x
        return 0
